patippuri
innūliṉ mūla āciriyar aṟiñar kilperṭ cilēṭṭar 1915 - 1922il tamiḻnāṭṭil (aṉṟiya ceṉṉimākāṇat teṉpakuti) ceṉṉippalkalik kaḻakattiṉ intiyap poruḷātārat tuṟiyiṉ mutal pērāciriyarāy,talivarāyp paṇiceytavar. poruḷātāram maṭṭumiṉṟi, varalāṟu,moḻiyiyal, camūkaviyal, paṇpāṭu mutaliya paltuṟikaḷilum vallavar.
tamiḻ - tamiḻar āyvi mēṟkoḷvārkkup peritum utavuvatākiyinnūl, empatippakam ivvāṇṭu veḷiyiṭum putiya nūlkaḷil oṉṟākum.itaṉi empatippaka nūlāciriyarkaḷuḷ oruvarākiya aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉcemmiyāka, nayamākat tamiḻākkiyuḷḷār.
intiyat tuṇikkaṇṭattiṉ nākarikattil, tirāviṭap (tamiḻiya)paṇpāṭṭu k kūṟuka ḷ aṉittut tuṟikaḷilum mukkāṟ pakutikkumatikamāka (twelve annas in the rupee) uḷḷaṉa eṉpār cunītikumārcaṭṭarji. itaṉik kaṭanta 90āṇṭu (1924-2014) kālamcār palatuṟiāyvukaḷ mēlum valuvāka niṟuviyuḷḷati, pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷuṭiya“cilēṭṭariṉ muṭivukaḷ kaṭanta 90 āṇṭukaḷil mēlum valuvaṭintuḷḷami”paṟṟiyatāṉa ceytikaḷi nūṉmukattil kāṇalām.
“intiya nākarika uruvākkattil tirāviṭa (tamiḻiya) moḻi pēcunarpaṅku: maṟupārvi tēvi” eṉpatu kuṟitta aṟiñar āntri ep. jōparkkuammiyār 1990 kaṭṭuri (innūliṉ pak: 119-165) innūṟ karuttukaḷivirivāka ātarippatik kāṇalām. tamiḻ-tamiḻar āyvi mēṟkoḷpavarkaḷukkup perum payaṉtaravalla innūli veḷiyiṭuvatilperumippaṭukiṟōm.
patippāḷar

iṇippu :
intiya nākarika uruvākkattil tirāviṭa moḻi
pēcunar paṅku : maṟupārvi tēvi - āntri
ep. jōparkku : 1990 kaṭṭuri. 


kilperṭ slēṭṭar(1924)
muṉṉuri
1915-ām āṇṭiṉ kōṭip paruvattiṉpōtu ceṉṉippalkalik kaḻakattiṉ āṭcik kuḻu eṉṉi intiyap poruḷātarattuṟiyiṉ (Indian Economics)talimikkup parinturittateṉṟuaṟivikkappaṭṭatum, nāṉ tamiḻ paṭikkat toṭaṅkiṉēṉ. appoḻutu‘i n ti ya nāka ri ka t ti ṉ mūla mu ta l āriyaccārpāṉatu’ eṉṟu iṉṟuintiyāvilum airōppāvilum paraviyuḷḷa kōṭpāṭṭiṉ uḷḷārnta
valuvil eṉakku mutaṉmutal aiyamēṟpaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi vanta piṉ(nakaramipput tiṭṭa vakiyil ceṉṉiaracāṅkattiṉ aṟivuriyāḷarāyirunta). ec.vi. laṅkāsṭarik kaṇṭu kalanturiyāṭiyatil,ivviyaṅkaḷ piṉṉum vaḷarntaṉa. avar eṉakku mikac cuviyāṉa palatakavalkaḷit tantatuṭaṉ, civakaṅki paṟṟiya viyattaku niḻaṟpaṭat tokutioṉṟiyum eṉakkuk kāṭṭiṉār.
2. 1 92 2 iḷavēṉil va ri nāṉ teṉṉin ti yāvil taṅ ki yakālattil tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ tōṟṟam, intiya nākarikattil ataṉpaṅku paṟṟiyum, eṉ uḷḷam ōyātu cintittu vantatu. ittakiya ārvamkoṇṭirunta piṟari nāṉ kaṇṭāl, avarkaḷuṭaṉum vātiṭuvēṉ.ivvāṟutāṉ innūl karuttukkaḷ eṉ eṇṇattil uruvāyiṉa. 1922-ilnāṉ iṅkilāntukkut tirumpic cellum varum vari eliyaṭ cumit, periākiyavarkaḷ nūlkaḷum māntar iṉattōṟṟa ārāycci, tolliyaltuṟikaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭup paravutaṟ koḷki cārnta nūlkaḷum eṉakkutteriya varavilli. intiyāvukku eṉ poruḷātārattuṟi cārnta nūlkaḷtavira maṟṟap putiya nūlkaḷ eṉṉi aṭiyavilli.
3. innūliṉ mutal nili varivi intiyāvil 1920-ileḻutiṉēṉ. ataṉi niṟivu paṭuttuvataṟkāṉa tūṇṭutal tantavarkaḷāṉa ṭākṭar ī.ī. pavar(Power), pērāciriyar puḷūr (Fleure)ākiyōrukkuk kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. mēlum taṭṭaccuppaṭi accuttiruttappaṭikaḷi vācittu putuk karuttukkaḷit tantutaviya pērāciriyarkaḷpuḷūr, eliyaṭ cumit(Elliott smith) ākiyōrukkum nāṉ pēraḷavu kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. attakiya pala karutturikaḷuḷ cila maṭṭumēavarkaḷuṭiya karuttukaḷ eṉa teḷivākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.4. mutal iyal cārnta puḷūr kuṟippil oru ciṟu ceytiyi nāṉ

intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷ
muḻumiyāka ēṟka iyalavilli. paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ paraviya vakiyili n tiyāviyum mesepoṭṭēmiyāviyum avar iṇittapōtilum, kaṭalvāṇikatti viṭat tari vāḻi vāṇikamē mukkiya kāraṇameṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. mottattil kaṭal neṟiyē mukkiyattuvammikkatāyirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ. itu ōraḷavuakkāla nilimi kāraṇamākavē āyiṉum, “kaṭaṟkari piṉ vāṅkiyapiṉṉar eritu nakariṉ vāḻvu ciṟuttup pōyviṭṭatu” eṉṟa varalāṟṟucceytiyiyum ātāramākak koṇṭatu eṉatu muṭivu. ki.mu. 3000-2000 kālaaḷavil tarivaḻi vāṇikatti viṭak kaṭal vāṇikat toṭarpēmukkiya māyiruntatu eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟa iyalāviṭiṉum ataṟkēmutaṉmi taralām. pāracīkakkuṭāvik kaṭantapiṉ, kaṭalvaḻippāti iraṇṭākap pirintu mēṟku nōkkiya kiḷi arēpiyāviṉ kaṭaṟkarivaḻiyē ceṉṟatu. aṭutta kiḷi kiḻakku teṟkākavum intiyak kaṭaṟkariōramākac ceṉṟatu. iraṇṭu kiḷikaḷumē camamukkiyattuvam vāyntirukkalām.
5. ekiptukkum intiyāvukkum iṭiyilāṉa kaṭalvaḻit toṭarpukku puḷūr avarkaḷiviṭa eliyaṭ cumittip piṉ paṟṟi atika mukkiyattuvam tarukiṟēṉ. avarkaḷiṭam iruntu caṟṟu vēṟupaṭṭu ivvāṇikattoṭarpu intiyappaṇpāṭṭi am mēṟṟici nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭuceṉṟirukkalām eṉṟu karutuvatilum, āriyar varavukku muṉṉarēintiyāvil valuvuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkivanta tirāviṭa nākarikam ammēṟṟici nāṭukaḷukkum paraviyirukkavāyppuṇṭu ākiyālataṟkum atika mutaṉmi taravēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu nilippāṭu ākum.


nūṉmukam
[1924 - 2014]: toṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷil ittuṟi āyvilnikaḻntuḷḷavaṟṟiṉ curukkam. [mēlum pala takavalkaḷuṭaṉ itivirivāka viḷakkuvatu iṇippil kāṇum virivāṉa āntrē ep. jōperkukaṭṭuri]
iṉṟu intiya nākarikam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭum nākarikak kūṟukaḷil perumpālāṉavi uṇmiyil tamiḻar, tamiḻiya nākarikak kūṟukaḷē “intiya nākarikattiṉ aṭittaḷam tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi - paṇpāṭu ākiyaviyē”
- es. ē. ṭilar 1973
All of Indian civilisation is built on an underlying base ofDravidian language and culture - S.A. Tyler (1973) India an Anthropological Perspective
iṅkilāntiluḷḷa palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷil paṇiyāṟṟiya piṉṉarāṅkilēya aṟiñar, kilperṭ cilēṭṭar 1915-23 kāla aḷavil ceṉṉippalkalikkaḻakattil intiyap poruḷiyal Indian Economics tuṟiyiāyvuttuṟiyākat toṭaṅki nilip paṭuttiṉār. 1916 - 17 il cumār pattuteṉṉintiyak kirāmaṅkaḷil(pala iṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭil) poruḷiyal kaḷāyvu ceytu 1918 il Some South Indian Villages eṉṉum āyvu nūliveḷiyiṭṭār. [piṉṉar atēkirāmaṅkaḷil pi.jē. tāmas mīṇṭum kaḷāyvu ceytu 1940il aṉṟiya niliyi viḷakkum āyvi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.]
2. ivar paltuṟi vallunar. iṅkilāntiṟku pōṉa piṉṉar 1931il SevenShakespeares eṉṟa nūli eḻutiyavar [es. muttiyā THEHINDU nāḷitaḻ 20.2.2012 kaṭṭuri]. poruḷiyal kuṟittu aintāṟunūlkaḷi eḻutiyuḷḷār. 1923 il ivar eḻutiya intiyap paṇpāṭṭiltirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak ] kūṟukaḷ The Dravidian element in Indian Culturenūl (āṅkilattil 192 pakkaṅkaḷ) pukaḻpeṟṟatākum. (i) intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak (tamiḻiyak) kūṟukaḷpaṟṟiya slēṭar 1924 muṭivukaḷik kaṭanta 90 āṇṭukaḷiṉpaltuṟi āyvukaḷ mēlum valuvāka ātarikkum nilimi
3. 1856 - 1980 kāla kaṭṭattilēyē kālṭuvel maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, ñāṉap pirakācar, ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar mutaliyōr niṟuviya tirāviṭa



moḻikkuṭumpat toṉmiyi iṉṟu paltuṟi aṟiñarum (scholars ofvarious disciplines of Sciences and Humanities like Human Palentology,Human Paleogenetics, Comparative and Historical Linguistics - includingNostratic and Mother Tongue studies,) ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. atumaṭṭumallatamiḻiya moḻi pēcunariṉ toṉmi, intiyāvilum teṉṉintiyāvilum iṉṟikku muṉṉar (i.mu = Before Present; B.P.) 50000 -10000 varic celkiṟatu. muntu tamiḻiyattiṉ toṉmiyāṉatu intōairōppiyam mutaliya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ tāyāṉa nāstirātik ,yūrēṣiyāṭik varic celkiṟatu. kāṇka: Chronology of the Major partof the world’slanguages accepted by scholars of Nostratic and MotherTongue studies including A.R. Bomhard Reconstructing Proto - NostraticBrill. Leiden (2008)
4. tamiḻiya moḻikkuṭumpattiṉ toṉmi teṉṉintiyāvilumintiyāvilum imu. 70000 - 50000 aḷavukku muntiyatu eṉpati iṉṟunam kālattil āṇittaramāka niṟuvi varupavarkaḷil mutaṉmiyāṉavaramerikka aṟiñar stīpaṉ hilyar leviṭ (1943-) mēli intōairōppiya moḻikaḷilum, tol intō - airōppiyam, nāstirātikiṉṟum kāṇappaṭum pala tamiḻiya moḻiyiyal kūṟukaḷi niṟuviyuḷḷār.ñā. tēvanēyap pāvaṇariṉ moḻiyiyal nōkkāṉatu ēṟkat takkateṉanilināṭ ṭi va ru m mu ta ṉmiyāṉa mēli moḻi yi ya la ṟi ña r . i va ramerikkāvil niyūyārkkil vāḻpavar. avar (International Journalof Dravidian Linguistics IJDL) 2007 kaṭṭuriyil terivittatukuṟippiṭattakkatu:-
āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷip paṟṟi āyvuceyta aṟiñar ammakkaḷiṉmoḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ maṭṭumē toṭarpuḷḷaṉa eṉak kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar.uṟavumuṟi (Kinship) pūmarāṅ (vaḷitaṭi) payaṉpāṭu ākiyaviyum ammakkaḷukkum tirāviṭarukkum uḷḷa toṭarpik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 6000ai oṭṭiulakeṅkum kaṭal maṭṭam uyarntu nilappakutikaḷiṉ parappu curuṅkiya poḻutu ulakiṉpiṟa pakuti makkaḷuṭiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṭam iruntu tuṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar āstirēliyappaḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ. avarkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviliruntu āstirēliyā vukkup pulampeyarntatu ēṟattāḻa 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar irukkalām eṉkiṉṟaṉar
āstirēliya aṟiñar. itiliruntu kuṟintatu 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē tamiḻiya(tirāviṭa) moḻipēcunar intiyāvil iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.inta
(“tirāviṭar ēṟṟam”) kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar teṉṉintiyāvi
liruntu vaṭanāṭu celkiṉṟaṉar; piṉṉar aṅkiruntu pāracīkattiṟkum atit tāṇṭippiṟa nilappakutikaḷukkum celkiṉṟaṉar. anta piṟa nilappakutikaḷil mūla tirāviṭamoḻik kuṭumpattiliruntu pirintu urālik, altāyik, intō-airōppiyam ākiyamoḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvākiṉṟaṉa. intō-airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattukku
tirāviṭam tāyā? tamakkiyā? eṉpati iṉṟiya niliyil tiṭṭavaṭṭamākakkūṟa iyalātu.”
2013 cūṉ IJDL kaṭṭuriyil leviṭ muṭivukaḷ varumāṟu: tamiḻiya(tirāviṭa) moḻikaḷil nām kāṇum oliyaṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷ; oru oliyaṉukkuppakaramāka iṉṉoru oliyaṉip peytu poruḷ māṟṟam , putupporuḷterivippatu iviyellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil kaḻipaḻaṅkālattukkumuṉṉarē iruntatik kāṇalām. ammāṟṟaṅkaḷi nāstirātikniliyilēyē muntu tirāviṭattiṭam iruntu ki.mu. 45000kku muṉṉarpeṟṟuviṭṭa (intō - airōppiyam uḷḷiṭṭa) piṟa nāstirātikamoḻip peruṅkuṭumpa moḻikaḷil (Indo European and other languagefamilies coming under Nostratic Macrofamily ) iṉṟum nilipeṟṟuḷḷatik kāṇalām.
5. piṉlāntu nāṭṭaṟiñar hāṉu pāṉu akasti hakōlā tamatu2009 / 2011 nūl Lexical Affinities Between Tamil and Finnish (A Contributionto Nostratic studies from the angle of close Genetic affinitiesbetween the Dravidian and Uralic Languagefamilies ), nūlil 765 +373 colloppumikaḷi parō - emeṉō (1983) ṉunuṉucu maṟṟumcentamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal pērakara mutali (1985 - 2007) ivaṟṟiyumpayaṉpaṭutti āyvu ceytu tantuḷḷār. innūlil hakōlā veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa muṭivukaḷ varumāṟu:(i) 50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar uruvāṉatu ñāla mutaṉ moḻi (MotherTongue of Man ) anta ñāla mutaṉ moḻikku aṭutta niliyil tōṉṟiya moḻipperuṅ kuṭumpaṅkaḷil (Macrofamily)  oṉṟāṉa Pre - Proto Nostratic  (tolnāstrātik) mutaṉ mutalil teṉṉintiyāvil nilavi yirukkalām. ulakil iṉṟuḷḷatamiḻiyam, intō airōppiyam, cemittiyam, urāl - altāyik moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ



 (maṟṟuma ki.mu. 3000 aḷavil vaḻaṅkiya cumēriyam, elām ākiya moḻikaḷ) ivaṟṟiṉtāykku moḻiyiyalāḷar cūṭṭiyuḷḷa peyar nāstirātik / yurōciyāṭik eṉpatākum.sasṭāṉpōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattil 2000 aktōparil patrirāju kiruṣṇamūrttiyōṭu pēcumpoḻutu jōcap hec. kirīṉperku kūṟiyatu: “tirāviṭa
moḻikkuṭumpam yurēciyāṭikkiṉ makaḷ alla; cakōtari yākattāṉ iruntirukka
muṭiyum” Bh. Krishnamurthy (2003) The Dravidian languages.]
	“(ii) 1000 ((Before Present) BP) kṣaña ai oṭṭi tol - nāstirātik pēcunarteṉṉintiyāviliruntu vaṭakku nōkkip paraviya piṉṉar (tol tirāviṭa - tamiḻiya -moḻiyiṉar intiyā aḷavil niṟka) nāstirātikkiṉ ēṉiya piriviṉar miyak kiḻakku- miya āciyap pakutiyil 10000 - 8000 BP kāla aḷavil uṭaṉuṟinta piṉṉar, cilāyiram, āṇṭukaḷil cumēriyam, intō - airōppiyam, urāl - altāyik,cemittiyam ākiya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pirintu āciyāvil pala pakutikaḷukkum, ekiptumutaliyavaṟṟukkum paraviṉa.
“ (iii) 7,000 BP (= kimu 5000) yiliruntu intiyā muḻuvatum (cintu - pañcāpuṭpaṭa) tamiḻiya moḻi pēciyavarkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar.
“(ie) miyakkiḻakku - miya āciyap pakutiyil ki.mu. 6000 - 4000 aḷavilvāḻnta “intō - airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpa mūlamoḻiyi (Proto - Indo – European)pēciyavarkaḷiṭamiruntu kiḻakku nōkkip pirinta intō irāṉiyap piriviṉoru uṭkiḷiyākiya “vēta - camskiruta” moḻi pēcunar kimu 2500 - 1500 kālaaḷavil ciṟu eṇṇikkiyil vaṭamēṟku intiya (cintu - pañcāp) pakutikku vantuaṅku tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻi pēcunaruṭaṉ kalantu viṭṭaṉar.”
6. intap piṉpulattil teṉ āciyāvil kaḻi paḻaṅkālattil orupotuvāṉa mūtāti moḻi iruntirukka vēṇṭum. taṟpōtiya māntiṉam (cumār 1 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar) ulakeṅkum paravattoṭaṅkiya kāla kaṭṭamē toltirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālamākalām. (It may be a question of a very ancient common substratum inSouth Asia, pre -Dravidian going back even to the original peopling ofthe world) v‹wh® Colin P. Masica (Paper in The Year Book - for 2001of south Asian languages and Linguistics; New Delhi; Sage Publications)
7. (i) iṉṟu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa 700 kōṭi maṉitarkaḷumē (atāvatuintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷtirāviṭar, intō airōppiyar, maṅkōliyar, cemittiyar, nīkrōvar,amerikka intiyar ākiya aṉivarumē) hōmō cēpiyaṉcu (HomoSapiens or Anatomically Modern Humans)eṉṉum orē iṉatticcārntavarkaḷ. ivviṉam āppirikkāvil iṉṟikku oṉṟari laṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vāḻnta orē tāyiṭam allatu kuḻuviṭam iruntutōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟiya aṟiviyalāḷar aṉivarum ēṟṟa muṭivu.kāṇka[Nature (London) 3 May 2012 Vol. 485 pak.25; maṟṟum Map onp.11 of Cyril Aydon (2009) A brief history of Mankind: 1,50,000 years ofHuman history (London; Constable and Robinson] eṉiṉum ēṟattāḻamaṉitaṉi yotta ‘muṉmānta’ (Hominid) iṉaṅkaḷ kaṭanta 48 laṭcam āṇṭukaḷil tōṉṟic cila pala laṭca āṇṭu vāḻnta piṉṉar muṟṟilum aḻintoḻintuviṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ hōmō erakṭacu iṉṟikku muṉṉarBefore Present (i.mu. 17 laṭcam - ki.mu 50000) eṉṟa iṉamum aṭaṅkum.atu maṭṭumē āppirikkāvi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟip piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷilumparaviyiruntatu . krōmakṉāṉ maṉitaṉ, pīkiṅ maṉitaṉ, īṭalparkmaṉitaṉ, cāvaka maṉitaṉ, attirampākkam pāsil maṉitaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ(cumār 3 laṭcam āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar vāḻntavarkaḷ) inta hōmōerakṭcu vakiyic cārntavarkaḷē. airōppāvil 40000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ vari vāḻntu piṉṉar aṭiyōṭu aḻintu pōṉa niyāṇṭartal(Neandarthal) (kimu 5 laṭcam - 30000) iṉamum avvāṟē.
(ii) viññāṉikaḷ mēlum niṟuviyuḷḷatu: iṉṟikku 70,000 -50,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarttāṉ āppirikkāvi viṭṭu taṟkāla māntiṉam (AMH)pulam peyarntu ulakiṉ piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷukkup paraviyatueṉpatākum. avvāṟuparavumuṉṉarē māntaṉ mutaṉmoḻi Mother Tongueof Man uruvākiviṭṭatueṉṟu varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyalāḷar uṭpaṭa palatuṟiaṟiñarum karutukiṉṟaṉar.H.H. Hock and B.D. Joseph (2009: II Revised edition) “Languagehistory, Language change and Language relationships (Historical andComparative linguistics”Mouten de Gruyter: Berlin. p 474: “Languageoriginated about 1,00,000 -- 50000 Before Present.... by way of gesturalshift to oral channel... vocal sounds at first were emphasisive attachkilperṭ slēṭṭar ments” (cuṭṭukaḷiṉ mutaṉmi paṟṟi kālṭuvel, ñāṉappirakācar aṉṟēkarutiyatum atuvē)...
 	(iii) avvāṟu āppirikkāvi viṭṭup peyarntu paraviya māntappiriviṉaril mukkiyamāṉa oru pirivu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyāka (teṉṉintiyāeṉpatu akkāla kaṭṭattil kaṭalōra nilappakutiyāka irunta kaṇṭattiṭṭuContinental Shelf pakutiyum cērntatu) ceṉṟu cilar teṉṉintiyāvilēyēnilittiṭa; vēṟu cilar āstirēliyā vari kaṭalōra nilappakuti vaḻiyākametumetuvāka, atāvatu oru talimuṟikku oṉṟiraṇṭu mil vītam, ceṉṟaṭintaṉar. intiyak kari cārnta kaṇṭattiṭṭup pakutiyil atāvatu kariyōrak kaṭaṟpakutiyil (Continental Shelf) āḻkaṭalakaḻāyvu ceytāl itu paṟṟiya tolliyal cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkalāmeṉpar piḷemiṅ (2004). āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷakkaṇṭattil imu 50000 mutal uḷḷaṉa.
 (iv) iṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷuḷ avvāṟu iṉṟikku 70000-50000 āṇṭu muṉṉar āppirikkāvi viṭṭu nīṅki āstirēliyāvaric ceṉṟaṭinta māntariṉ piṟaṅkaṭikaḷ perumaḷavil uḷḷaṉareṉpati miṭakāṉṭiriyal DNA mānta marapaṇu āyvu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākaniṟuviviṭṭatu. avvāyvi mēṟkoṇṭa viññāṉikaḷ kuḻuvil maturiyiccārnta muṉivar irāmacāmi piccappaṉum oruvar. 23.8.2007, 16.4.2008nāḷkaḷil avar ceytittāḷ nērkāṇalkaḷil terivitta ceytikaḷ varumāṟu:-
“āppi ri kkāvi viṭṭu iṉṟikku 70000- 50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar veḷiyēṟiya oru kuḻuviṉariṭam mi DNA aṭiyāḷakkuṟiyīṭu M130 tōṉṟiyatu. iṉṟuḷḷa āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷtokiyiṉaruḷ iraṇṭil oruvariṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu.
“maturip pakutiyil uḷḷa makkaḷ palaruṭiya mi DNA-vi āyvuceytatil avarkaḷil 5liruntu 7 naparkaḷiṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu eṉṟuteriya vantatu. aṉṟiya kaṭaṟkarip pakuti vaḻiyāka mānta iṉattiṉ orupirivu āppirikkāviliruntu kiḻakku nōkkip pulam peyarntatu eṉpatiinta āyvu meyppittatu (Proceedings of theNational Academy ofSciences; USA ; 2001) piriṭṭaṉ nāṭṭu marapaṇuviyalaṟiñar car vālṭarpāṭmar coṉṉatu pōl. “ulakil iṉṟuḷḷa makkaḷ aṉivarum (700intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷkōṭi pērumē) āppirikkāviliruntu pulampeyarntu vantavarkaḷiṉpiṟaṅkaṭikaḷē.”
	‘intiya makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum mi DNA aṭiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭukaḷil mikat toṉmi vāyntatu M130 tāṉ. ativiṭap paḻimiyāṉami DNA kuṟiyīṭukaḷintiyāvil evariṭamum illi. āppirikkāvilikkāla mānta iṉam AMH uruvākiya piṉṉar akkaṇṭatti viṭṭumutalil pulam peyarntu intiyāvil mutalil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷiṉpiṟaṅkaṭikaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷaṉar eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
8. “(ki.mu 300 - ki.pi. 200 kālattic cārnta) caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷtamiḻaka maṇṇilēyē tol varavākat tōṉṟiyavi eṉpatik kāṭṭumaṉittu aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. enta nāṭṭiliruntō tamatu
moḻiyuṭaṉum ilakkiyattuṭaṉum tamiḻar tamiḻakattukku vantu kuṭiyēṟiyatākayātoru ātāramum annūlkaḷil illi eṉpatiyum itiliruntuuṇaralām.” eṉṟa cēviyartaṉi nāyakam (1953) muṭivu iṉṟu acikkamuṭiyātatāka uḷḷatu. slēṭariṉ iyal 1il tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarmecapotāmiyā pakutiyiliruntu ki.mu. 3000kkuppiṉ intiyāvukkuḷnuḻintu piṉṉart tamiḻnāṭu varip piṉṉar kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉṟuṉṉippu iṉṟu aṭipaṭṭuviṭṭatu. māṟāka, mikap paḻaṅ kālattil (ki.mu.5000kkum neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉar) mutaṟkaṇ kāvirik kariyiluruvākiya arappā nākarikam intiya mēṟkukkari vaḻiyākat tamiḻarkaḷālcintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu, naṇṇilakkari nāṭukaḷ vari koṇṭucellappaṭṭatu. eṉṟu - tiruttanti hīrās toṉmi intō -naṇṇilakkari nākarika āyvu (Studies in Indo Mediterranean Culture1953) nūlil niṟuviyuḷḷa muṭiviyē iṉṟiyap paltuṟi āyvukaḷumātarikkiṉṟaṉa.“tirāviṭa” moḻik kuṭumpamā? “tamiḻiya” moḻik
kuṭumpamā?
9. moḻiyiyalil tamiḻiya (Tamilian) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟēkālṭuvel kālam vari vaḻaṅkiya peyari avar tāṉ “tirāviṭa” moḻikkuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccit tiripuvaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatākum. makāvamcattil “tamiḷa”, taṇṭi eḻutiya   kilperṭ slēṭṭar avanticuntari kati yil “tramiḷa” eṉṟa collum varukiṉṟaṉa. ikṣvākukuṭimara piṉariṉ pirākirutak kalveṭṭilum ‘tamiḷa’ tāṉ varukiṟatu. piṉṉarkumārilapaṭṭariṉ tantiravārttikattil
“tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva”(appaṭi tirāviṭa (=tamiḻ) maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷil)eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ-tamiḷa-tramiḷa-tramiṭa-traviṭa-tirāviṭa eṉṟu
māṟiviṭṭatu. ē.ci. parṉel 1872 intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tantira vārttikap pakutiyi acciṭṭapoḻutu apattamāka,
“āntra trāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva”eṉṟu acciṭṭār (mēlum paṟpala piḻikaḷuṭaṉ). intat tavaṟāṉavācakatti kālṭuvel (1875) sṭeṉ kōṉō (Linguistic Survey ofIndia: 1906) pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar. ittavaṟi pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri 42ām tokutiyil (1913) teḷḷat teḷivāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. (kuññīṇṇirājā Annals of Oriental Research tokuti28 (1979) kaṭṭuriyil kuṟittuḷḷatu pōla, parṉel uṭiya tavaṟāṉa
vācakattit tamiḻaṟiñar palarum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟi “tamiḻiyum, teluṅkiyum orucērak kumārilapaṭṭar kuṟittār” eṉṟu tavaṟāka eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar.)
10. “tirāviṭam” eṉṉum collil iruntu “tamiḻ” uruvāṉatueṉṟu kālṭuvel tavaṟākak kuṟippiṭṭatu paṟṟi avarik kuṟi kūṟalollātu. akkālattil tamiḻ cārnta moḻiyiyal āyvukaḷ irunta niliavvaḷavutāṉ. āṉāl avarukkup piṉṉar 1887il kalittoki ppatippuriyil ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi toṭaṅki, kamil culepil (JourNal of the Institute of Asian Madras IV-2: 1987) muṭiya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa moḻiyiyalaṟiñar aṉivarum “tamiḻ” eṉṉum colliṉ tiripuṟṟavaṭivamē “tirāviṭam” eṉṟu āṇittaramāka niṟuviyuḷḷa niliyiliṉṟum “tirāviṭam” eṉṉum colliliruntu “tamiḻ” eṉṉum coluruvākiyatu eṉa kūṟikkoṇṭiruppatu aṟiyāmi.
11. toltirāviṭam (Proto-Dravidian) eṉṟu vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūlāyvāḷar mīṭṭuruvākkam ceyvaṉavaṟṟuḷ pala piḻiyāy muṭikiṉṟaṉeṉpatiyum, paḻantamiḻē itaṟkuk kaṭṭaḷik kallākak koḷḷappaṭavēṇṭum eṉpatiyum pāvāṇar niṟuviviṭṭār. tamiḻiṉ tiripukaḷē piṟintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ. paḻantamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭatāka tol tirāviṭam eṉpatu kaṟpaṉiyē eṉpar pāvāṇar. ākavē tirāviṭam, tol tirāviṭameṉa kuṟippiṭappaṭuvaṉa vellām uṇmiyil paḻantamiḻiyē kuṟippatākuṇarka. eṉiṉum ulaka aḷavil “tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam” eṉpatu orukuṟiyīṭṭuc col ākiviṭṭa niliyil ati nām payaṉpaṭuttuvatiltavaṟilli. ataṉ uṇmip poru ḷ ‘ ta mi ḻi ya moḻik ku ṭu mpam’eṉpatē.
12. ji.yū.pōp tirukkuṟaḷ āṅkilap peyarppi 1886il veḷiyiṭṭapoḻutu annūl muṉṉuriyil kūṟiyatu:-
“(tamiḻē) teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ tāy ākum. teṉṉintiya moḻik kuṭumpattit tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpam eṉṟu aḻippatē cari eṉṟu nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ. akkuṭumpa moḻikaḷil etaṉi āyvuceyya vēṇṭumeṉ ṟālum tamiḻmoḻip piṉṉaṇiyil tāṉ āyvuceyya vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu”
(i) aṇmi āṇṭukaḷil tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpattiṉ toṉmi- āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiyoppa - teṉṉintiyāvilum intiyā vilum i.mu. (iṉṟikku muṉṉar;Before Present) 50000 varic celkiṟatu.
(ii) attakiya toltamiḻiyak kuṭu mpattiliruntē intōairōppiyam, urāl, altāyk pōṉṟa moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pirintirukkavēṇṭum eṉa iṉṟu karutappaṭuvatālum, (teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam pōṉṟēṉiya tirāviṭamoḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟak kālatti eppaṭip pārttālum 2000āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉark koṇṭu cella iyalātu eṉpatālum) tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpam eṉṟu aḻippatē cari; eṉiṉummoḻiyiyalil kuṟiyīṭṭuccol ākiviṭṭa “tirāviṭa” moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟa peyarit toṭarntu payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭiyatākiṟatu.
 	13. el.vi. irāmacāmi aiyarum tamatu Educational Reviewcūli 1928 kaṭṭuriyil piṉvarumāṟu tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyi valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār:- “ ta mi ḻi ṉ  māperu m  coṟ ka ḷa ñ ci ya t ti l  u ḷ ḷa  coṟkaḷ  ovvoṉṟukkum teḷivāṉa tamiḻvērik kāṇamuṭiyum; iṉṟu vaḻaṅkumtamiḻccoṟkaḷilum mikappala cila āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākat tūya kilperṭ slēṭṭar tamiḻ vaṭivattil vaḻaṅkivantuḷḷaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilēyē tamiḻtāṉmūlamoḻiyiṉ muḻuttaṉmiyip perum aḷavukku iṉṟum koṇṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻil kāṇum eḷimiyāṉa teḷivāṉa vērkaḷiliruntutāṉcilapala (paṭimuṟiyāṉa) māṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ēṉiya tirāviṭamoḻikaḷiluḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷi viḷakka iyalukiṟatu.
“eṉavē inniliyil tamiḻccol etaṉiyum tūya tamiḻ ōraci,īraci vēriṉ / vērkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil viḷa k ka iyalumpoḻutu (tamiḻil ivvāṟu viḷakkum poḻutu kiṭṭum arumiyāṉa teḷiviyumoḷiyiyum kuḻappum vakiyil) ac collukku tol tirāviṭa ProtoDravidian vaṭivatti uṉṉippatu utavākkari vēli. iṅkoṉṟumaṅkoṉṟumāka cila nērvukaḷil maṭṭumē piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil uḷḷaciṟcila coṟkaḷi viḷakkuvataṟkutoltirāviṭaccol vaṭivaṅkaḷi uṉṉippatu tēvippaṭum.”
Considering the fact that Tamil’s vast word-hoard couldbe traced to its own elementary native roots and further that someof the most ancient forms (so far as we know) have been handeddown to us in their pristine nature, Tamil represents within certinlimits the most conservative of Dravidian dialects. This viewreceives confirmation from the fact that many of the elementaryroots of Tamil are found (with modifications and alterations ofvarious degrees) in a large number of other dialects
“In this view then, if a Tamil word could be satisfactorilyexplined as being normally derived from a native Tamil root itself,or a combination of roots, it would be futile to constructproto-forms of words, obscuring the relationships of root andword in Tamil, unless indeed the affinities of Tamil words androots with those of other dialects demand it.”
- L.V. Ramaswami Iyer



 (ii) tol tamiḻiyac cintu nākarikam
14. 1923 ām āṇṭil eḻuti muṭittu 1924 il veḷivantatuslēṭariṉ innūl. āṉāl 1923kkup piṉṉart tāṉ car jāṉ mārṣaltalimiyil mokeñcotarō vilum harappāvilum akaḻvāyvukaḷintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷtoṭaṅkappaṭṭu mutaṟkaṭṭa muṭivukaḷ 1924 ceptempariltāṉ mārṣal Il-Lustrated London News kaṭṭuriyil veḷiyiṭṭār. annākarikam tamiḻiya(tirāviṭa) nākarikam (ki.mu. 7000 - 1800); annākarika muttirikaḷ tamiḻiyamoḻiyē eṉpati virivāka pi. irāmanātaṉ (2012) tol tamiḻiyac cintunākarikam nūlil kāṇalām. cintunākaric ceytikaḷ vanta piṉṉar eḻutiyiruntāl intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak kūṟukaḷi mēlum valuvākattam nūlil cilēṭṭar niṟuviyiruppār.
(ii) hīrās tamatu 1953 “Studies in Proto Indo - MediterraneanCulture”nūliṉ aṟimukattil piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār:
mikap paḻaṅkālattil (ki.mu. 5000kkum neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉar)mutaṟkaṇ kāvirik kariyil uruvākiya arappā nākarikam intiyamēṟkukkari vaḻiyākat tamiḻarkaḷāl cintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu,naṇṇilakkari nāṭukaḷ vari koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu.
(intō āriya moḻi pēcunariṉ (ciṟu eṇṇikkiyil) nuḻivukkumuṉṉar) intiyāveṅkum tirāviṭamoḻi pēcunarē vacittu vantaṉar.(heviṭ 1889; hāl 1913, rikvētam upaniṣattukkaḷ kālattiliruntētirāviṭat tākkam (viṉik koḷki, maṟupiṟappu, yōkam) uḷḷatu. vēta,purāṇap pakutikaḷil pala paṇṭit tirāviṭa nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppukaḷē, (hīrācukku 22.11.1942 aṉṟu eḻutiya kaṭitattil vi.es. cuktaṅkaryutiṣṭiraṉ kati āriyar varavukkum rik vētattukkum muntiyatu eṉpatucariyāṉa karuttē’ eṉat terivittār.) nākarikamaṟṟa niliyil intiyāviṟkuḷ nuḻinta intō āriyar tamatu moḻiyi camskirutamāka(tiruntiya moḻiyāka) ākkikkoṇṭaṉar. (They converted their rudematter of -course speech -- a speech of shepherds and husbandmen -into a classical Sanskrit language)
tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar naṇṇilakkari nāṭukaḷiliruntu intiyāviṟkum teṉintiyāviṟkum vantavarkaḷ allar. iṅkiruntu aṅkuceṉṟu piṉṉar ulakeṅkum nākarikattip parappiyarvarkaḷ ivarkaḷē.ittuṟi āyvukaḷ niṟivaṭiyum poḻutu tirāviṭa nākarikamākiya cintunākarikam ulaka nākarikat tiṉ toṭṭil eṉpatu ēṟkappaṭum.“the Dravidians of India, after a long period of development inthis country, travelled westwards, and settling successively in the various lands, they found their way from Mesopotamia upto the Britishisles, spread theirrace -- afterwards named Mediterranean owing to theplace where they were known anthropologically -- through the west andmade their civilization flourish in two continents, being thus the originatorsof the modern world civilization. The Mediterranean nations of theancient world were racial off-shoots of the mighty proto - Indian race”[After the problems of decipherment of Indus script and those ofthe migration of Dravidian civilisation out of India to Elam, Sumeria andthe West are solved]India will be acknowledged as the cradle of humancivilization.
15. eṉ. lākōvari 1963il veḷiyiṭṭa tirāviṭar tōṟṟamum mēlnāṭukaḷum (Dravidian Origins and the West) eṉṉum nūl ciṟantamoḻiyiyal meymikaḷi uṇarttuvatākum:-

(8) viṉic collil ōr uyireḻutti nīṭṭi olittu viṉiyālaṇiyum
peyarkaḷi amittal.
“
“Primitive underived Indo-Europeanisms discoverable in theDravidian languages (in current parlance: ‘proto-Indo European featuresderived from Dravidian”):-
1.The use of n, as in Greek to prevent hiatus.”
2. The existence of gender in the pronouns of the third person,
and in verbs, and in particular the existence of a neuter gender.
3. The use of d or t as the sign of the neuter singular of demonstrative
pronouns of the third person.
4. The existence of a neuter plural, as in Latin, in short a.
5. The formation of the remote demonstrative from a base in a; the proximate from the base in i.
6. The formation of most preterites, as in Persian by the addition of d.
7. The formation of some preterites by the reduplication of aportion of the root.
8. The formation of a considerable number of verbal nouns by
lengthening the vowel of the verbal root.”
- irāparṭ kālṭuvel

(avarkaḷiṭam piṉṉar uruvāṉaviyē kirīk,lattīṉ, kelṭik, cermāṉik, cilāviya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷākum) vēṟucila kuḻukkaḷ kiḻakku-teṉkiḻakku nōkki iṭam peyarnta kālam

24. i ṉ ṟiya “ i n tu ” camayak kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭ ṭu muṟikaḷ, meyyiyal ākiyavi muṟṟilum tamiḻiya (tirāviṭac) cārpuṭiyaviyē;āriyariṭamiruntu peṟṟavi alla eṉpati 1900kku muṉṉar iruntēnallaṟiñar kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (1903: mullippāṭṭuārāycciyuri , 1923 tamiḻar nākarikam , 1941 tamiḻar matam); pi.ṭi. cīṉivācaiyaṅkār (1929: History of the Tamils (1940tamiḻar camayam) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (1972: tamiḻar matam ) ākiyōr uṭpaṭa.āriya vētaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa, avaṟṟukku muntiya ‘tamiḻ nāṉmaṟikaḷ’ iruntaṉa veṉṉum kōṭpāṭṭiyum kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi(1927; The Madras Christian College Magazine; piṉṉar 1920(centamiḻccelvi) mutaliyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar.
2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟi - cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi.caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putuk kaṟkālak karuvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷnāṉku uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi ai. makātēvaṉ “murukuaṉ” (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār.akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2000-1500āka irukkalām eṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. “putukkaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orēmoḻiyiyē pēciṉar eṉṟum ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē eṉṟum ikkaṇṭu piṭippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpar ai. makātēvaṉ.” (“The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu andthe Indus valley people shared the same language which can only beDravidian and not Indo-Aryan):1.5.2006 intu nāḷitaḻ.
25. iṉṟiya vētamatak karuttukkaḷ - avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa camaṇa, puttammutaliya karuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟku iṭiyē mutalil uṟaḻvu, piṉṉar iṇippueṉṟavāṟu intumatam uruvāṉatu eṉṟu karutuvatu avvaḷavu cariyāṉatalla. cintuveḷi nākarikam cārnta, paṇṭiya tollintiya (tamiḻiya) nākarikattiṉ puttuyirppē,maṟumalarcciyē iṉṟiya intu matam ākum. (The Hindu synthesis was less thedialectical reductionof orthodoxy and heterodoxy than the resurgenceof the ancient aboriginal Indus civilisation)”  eṉpar ṭilar (1973)
26. “paḻiya upaniṭataṅkaḷil maṟupiṟavik koḷki putumiyāṉatākavum
cilarukku maṭṭum terinta maṟi poruḷākavum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭu iruntatu. iruntum
piṉṉar akkoḷki putta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka virintu aṉivarālum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu evvāṟu? enta makkaḷ camūkattiṉariṭam iruntu puttar tōṉṟiṉārō, avarkaḷiṭiyē akkoḷki iruntu irukka vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāṉ atu virivilaṉittu makkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.”
